_source line 799 · 2026-05-25T16:55:01.725332+00:00_

1	\section{Discussion and Conclusion}
2	\label{sec:conclusion}
3	
4	The dominant model$\times$item interaction (30--36\%) reveals that model rankings are partly item-dependent: models disagree on which items are difficult, not merely on overall ability. We echo calls for item-level reporting \cite{polo2024tinybenchmarks} and further recommend that benchmark developers release item-level metadata to support such analyses.
5	
6	The exploratory format-associated pattern, with MC benchmarks concentrating 68--72\% in item-related components versus 31--41\% for FF ($p = 0.10$), suggests that evaluation reliability depends on response format. Item difficulty heterogeneity ($\rho = 0.82$) provides a candidate mechanism, with MC items concentrating variance in the item facet while FF items distribute it across items, models, and prompts. If confirmed with broader benchmark samples, item budgets should be calibrated per format rather than universally.
7	
8	A 3-prompt $\times$ 3-seed design reduces items from 552 to 178 for $G \geq 0.80$ on MMLU; cross-model projections yield 93--128 items versus 1,078--2,768 single-condition (Table~\ref{tab:dstudy_cross}). Since G-theory protocol optimization and IRT item selection are orthogonal, combining both could further reduce evaluation costs.
9	
10	We recommend three practices for LLM evaluation: (1)~adopt multi-facet designs (minimally 3 prompts $\times$ 3 seeds) for published results; (2)~report item-level accuracy to expose model$\times$item interaction; and (3)~calibrate item budgets to benchmark format. We release an open-source G-theory pipeline and D-study budget calculator to facilitate adoption.

104	Our target universe comprises open-weight, instruction-tuned models in the 7--9B parameter range (Table~\ref{tab:model_ids}); results may not generalize to API-based, base, or $>$9B models. The eight models are treated as a quasi-random facet \cite{meredith1993measurement, putnick2016measurement}. We report $G$ under random-model assumptions, the more conservative specification: treating models as fixed yields $G_\text{fixed}$ 0.951--0.990 vs.\ $G_\text{random}$ 0.863--0.896 (increase of 0.077--0.113; Table~\ref{tab:fixed_vs_random}), because model$\times$item interaction (30--36\%) enters relative error variance. Consequently, D-study budgets are upper bounds. LOMO analysis preserves component rankings across all five benchmarks (Appendix~\ref{app:robustness}); four-model subset checks match within $|\Delta G| = 0.035$ (\S\ref{sec:dstudy_budgets}).
105	\label{sec:model_facet}

18	\paragraph{Facet specification sensitivity.} Treating temperature and ordering as fixed (rather than random) facets changes $G_\text{item}$ by only 0.37 percentage points (all-random: 0.820 vs.\ fixed temperature+ordering: 0.824). The small difference reflects the negligible temperature$\times$item (0.65\%) and ordering$\times$item (0.54\%) variance shares. The random-facet assumption is therefore robust to facet classification.
19	
20	\paragraph{Leave-one-model-out (LOMO).} Across all 40 leave-one-model-out subsets (8 models $\times$ 5 benchmarks), $G_\text{item}$ ranges from 0.833 to 0.919, with CV $\leq$ 1.25\% per benchmark. No single model removal changes $G$ by more than 0.030 (GSM8K, removing Gemma-2-9B). Item-related variance rankings are preserved in all 40 subsets. Table~\ref{tab:lomo_full} (main text) reports per-benchmark stability. This supports treating models as exchangeable within the target universe of 7--9B instruction-tuned models (\S\ref{sec:model_facet}).
21	
22	\paragraph{Inter-model agreement.} We computed pairwise Cohen's $\kappa$ and Fleiss' $\kappa$ across all model pairs on each benchmark. Mean pairwise $\kappa$ ranges from 0.326 (MATH) to 0.461 (MMLU); Fleiss' $\kappa$ ranges from 0.309 (GSM8K) to 0.456 (MMLU). HellaSwag uses six models ($\kappa = 0.332$) due to missing OLMo and Yi data. The fair-to-moderate agreement independently confirms that the model$\times$item interaction (30--36\% of variance) reflects genuine differential item functioning: models disagree substantially on which items are difficult, consistent with prior DIF findings in educational measurement \cite{holland1993dif, clauser1998dif}.
23

1	\begin{figure*}[t]
2	\centering
3	\includegraphics[width=\textwidth]{fig_overview_pipeline}
4	\caption{Overview of the G-theory evaluation pipeline. A factorial design crossing seven facets across eight models and five benchmarks produces 2.3M binary records. G-study variance decomposition via Henderson Method~I reveals that model$\times$item interaction accounts for up to 36\% of variance. D-study projections translate variance components into design-dependent item budgets: a 3-prompt $\times$ 3-seed design achieves $G \geq 0.80$ with 178 items (3.1$\times$ reduction). Three contributions emerge: reproducibility budgets (C1), dominant model$\times$item interaction (C2), and format-associated variance patterns (C3).}
5	\label{fig:overview}
6	\end{figure*}

23	Extending to eight models (BF16 subset, 460,800 records), Table~\ref{tab:cross_model} shows that item difficulty drops from 58.4\% to 37.9\%, while model$\times$item interaction emerges at 30.2\% (up to 36\% across benchmarks, \S\ref{sec:format_conditional}). The model main effect is 3--5\% (MC) and 9--11\% (FF)\footnote{Ranges rounded to nearest integer; exact values are 2.8--5.4\% (MC) and 8.5--10.7\% (FF) per Table~\ref{tab:cross_benchmark}.}, suggesting that models differ primarily in \emph{which items} they find difficult rather than in overall ability level. Figure~\ref{fig:item_heatmap} visualizes this pattern across models and benchmarks. Leave-one-model-out (LOMO) analysis (Table~\ref{tab:lomo_full}) confirms this structure is stable: no single model removal changes $G$ by more than 0.030 across all five benchmarks. Inter-model agreement independently quantifies this pattern: pairwise Cohen's $\kappa$ ranges from 0.326 (MATH) to 0.461 (MMLU), indicating only fair-to-moderate agreement on individual items despite similar aggregate accuracy, consistent with models differing in \emph{which items} they solve rather than in overall ability (Appendix~\ref{app:robustness}).

93	\paragraph{Cross-benchmark $G$ stability.}
94	\label{app:cross_benchmark_transfer}
95	$G_\text{item}$ values cluster in a narrow band across all five benchmarks (0.863--0.896, CV = 1.4\%), despite qualitatively different variance structures: MC benchmarks concentrate 68--72\% in item-related components while GSM8K disperses variance across item (17\%), model (11\%), and interaction (15\%) sources (Table~\ref{tab:cross_benchmark}). This stability implies that D-study budget recommendations calibrated on one benchmark provide reasonable order-of-magnitude guidance for others. However, the 0.033 range in $G_\text{item}$ translates to a 71--128 item range for multi-facet $G \geq 0.80$ (Table~\ref{tab:dstudy_cross}), and the variance compositions differ substantially; benchmark-specific G-studies remain necessary to identify dominant variance sources and optimize condition selection.
96	
97	\section{Format-Associated Variance: Per-Benchmark Details}

_source line 835 · 2026-05-25T16:56:05.505739+00:00_

1	\section{Conclusion}
2	\label{sec:conclusion}
3	
4	The dominant non-residual variance source in LLM evaluation is the model$\times$item interaction (30--36\% in MC, 15--17\% in FF), revealing that models disagree on which items are difficult rather than differing uniformly in overall ability. Multi-facet evaluation designs absorb this interaction: a 3-prompt $\times$ 3-seed protocol achieves $G \geq 0.80$ with 178 MMLU items versus 552 under single-condition, with cross-benchmark budgets of 93--128 items. The variance structure is format-dependent: MC benchmarks concentrate 68--72\% in item-related components while FF benchmarks disperse to 31--41\%, motivating per-format budget calibration. We recommend three practices: adopt multi-facet designs for published results, report item-level accuracy to expose model$\times$item interaction, and calibrate item budgets per benchmark format.
5

### Tool result

_source line 837 · 2026-05-25T16:56:08.919407+00:00_

1	LLM benchmarks are the primary tool for comparing language models, guiding decisions about deployment, funding, and public rankings. Every evaluation involves design choices (prompt template, random seed, numerical precision, answer ordering) that are typically fixed to a single value and assumed inconsequential. Growing evidence challenges this assumption: prompt formatting alone can shift accuracy by up to 76 percentage points, and answer ordering can flip model rankings. Yet each study examines one factor in isolation, leaving unanswered which sources dominate when all vary simultaneously, and how many test items are truly needed for reliable measurement. We address this using Generalizability theory, a psychometric framework for multi-source variance decomposition. In a factorial experiment crossing seven facets across eight open-weight models and five benchmarks (2.3M binary records), we find that the dominant non-residual variance source is not the prompt or the seed but the model$\times$item interaction (30--36\% in multiple-choice benchmarks, 15--17\% in free-form), meaning models disagree on which items are difficult rather than in overall ability. Multi-facet designs absorb this noise, reducing item requirements from over 1,000 to roughly 100. The variance structure is format-dependent: item factors dominate multiple-choice benchmarks but disperse in free-form tasks. We release an open-source G-theory pipeline and D-study calculator\footnote{Code and data are available at \url{https://anonymous.4open.science/r/nlp_variance_gtheory-11E1/}.}.
2

_source line 840 · 2026-05-25T16:56:31.762522+00:00_

1	\section{Discussion and Conclusion}
2	\label{sec:conclusion}
3	
4	The dominant non-residual variance source---the model$\times$item interaction (30--36\% in MC benchmarks, 15--17\% in FF)---reveals that model rankings are partly item-dependent: models disagree on which items are difficult, not merely on overall ability. We echo calls for item-level reporting \cite{polo2024tinybenchmarks} and further recommend that benchmark developers release item-level metadata to support such analyses.
5	
6	The exploratory format-associated pattern, with MC benchmarks concentrating 68--72\% in item-related components versus 31--41\% for FF ($p = 0.10$), suggests that evaluation reliability depends on response format. Item difficulty heterogeneity ($\rho = 0.82$) provides a candidate mechanism, with MC items concentrating variance in the item facet while FF items distribute it across items, models, and prompts. If confirmed with broader benchmark samples, item budgets should be calibrated per format rather than universally.
7	
8	A 3-prompt $\times$ 3-seed design reduces items from 552 to 178 for $G \geq 0.80$ on MMLU; cross-model projections yield 93--128 items versus 1,078--2,768 single-condition (Table~\ref{tab:dstudy_cross}). Since G-theory protocol optimization and IRT item selection are orthogonal, combining both could further reduce evaluation costs.
9	
10	We recommend three practices for LLM evaluation: (1)~adopt multi-facet designs (minimally 3 prompts $\times$ 3 seeds) for published results; (2)~report item-level accuracy to expose model$\times$item interaction; and (3)~calibrate item budgets to benchmark format. We release an open-source G-theory pipeline and D-study budget calculator to facilitate adoption.
11

1	\section{Conclusion}
2	\label{sec:conclusion}
3	
4	The dominant non-residual variance source in LLM evaluation is the model$\times$item interaction (30--36\% in MC, 15--17\% in FF), revealing that models disagree on which items are difficult rather than differing uniformly in overall ability. Multi-facet evaluation designs absorb this interaction: a 3-prompt $\times$ 3-seed protocol achieves $G \geq 0.80$ with 178 MMLU items versus 552 under single-condition, with cross-benchmark budgets of 93--128 items. The variance structure is format-dependent: MC benchmarks concentrate 68--72\% in item-related components while FF benchmarks disperse to 31--41\%, motivating per-format budget calibration. We recommend three practices: adopt multi-facet designs for published results, report item-level accuracy to expose model$\times$item interaction, and calibrate item budgets per benchmark format.
5

1	\section*{Limitations}
2	
3	Our design covers eight 7--9B instruction-tuned models; preliminary 72B validation confirms consistent variance structure, but broader scale and temporal coverage is needed. The benchmark sample ($n = 5$) limits statistical power for format-associated observations ($p = 0.10$). All D-study budgets assume binary scoring; partial-credit analysis on GSM8K shows $G$ drops from 0.86 to 0.77, suggesting limited transferability to finer-grained rubrics. Benchmark contamination in pretraining data may inflate item accuracy and compress variance; our design does not control for this source.
4

_source line 885 · 2026-05-25T19:40:39.392541+00:00_

[飞书用户 用户]: 📊 论文自检任务（重大）：全文数据真实性与可追溯性彻底审计

这是一次彻底的大检查，必须认真对待。AI 写论文最致命的两个问题：编数字（hallucinate）和抄数字（数字之间不一致或来源不明）。这次的目标是把每一个出现在论文里的数字都"对账"清楚。

⚠️ 凡是查不到出处的数字，要么找到出处，要么删掉。不允许凭印象、凭"差不多"、凭老版本残留的数字留在论文里。

---

审计范围（无遗漏）：

1. 图（Figures）
   - 每张图里的数据点、bar 高度、曲线值、error bar
   - 图的 caption 里出现的数字（"reduces by 23%"、"on average 4.2"）
   - 坐标轴 tick 标注

2. 表（Tables）
   - 主结果表、ablation 表、超参表、基线表
   - 单元格里的每个数字，包括精度（小数位数）
   - 表头里的尺寸说明（"N=1000"、"k=5"）

3. 正文（Body）
   - Abstract 里的关键 claim 数字
   - Introduction 的 contribution 数字
   - Method 描述里的超参（learning rate、batch、steps、模型尺寸、参数量）
   - Experimental setup（数据集大小、训练 GPU 小时数、wall-clock 时间）
   - Analysis 段落里逐一引用的数字
   - Conclusion 里复述的数字

4. 附录（Appendix）
   - 完整的超参表、额外实验结果
   - 数据集详细统计
   - 失败案例的统计
   - Proof 里的常数、bound

---

对每个数字，逐个确认这 3 个问题：

Q1（真实性）：这个数字是从一次 真实运行 的实验里出来的吗？

合格证据：
- 在 results.yaml / 实验日志 / WandB run 里能定位到原始记录
- registry 里有对应的实验 ID
- 知道是 seed N 跑的、还是 mean of N seeds

不合格：
❌ "之前跑过一版差不多是这个数"
❌ "我们估算的"（除非论文明确说明 "estimated"）
❌ "上次 AI 写的时候放进来的，我也不知道哪来的"
❌ "占位符忘删了"

Q2（正确性）：抄到论文里有没有抄错？

- 原始记录 73.18% 抄到表里有没有变成 73.81% 或 73.2%？
- 小数位精度跟原始一致吗？（不要四舍五入到误差范围以外）
- 单位对吗（accuracy vs F1，pp vs %，BLEU vs ROUGE）

Q3（可追溯性）：数字写在论文里几个地方，每个地方都一致吗？

同一个指标可能出现在：Abstract、Introduction contribution、Method、Main Table、Analysis 段落、Conclusion —— 必须逐字逐位一致。不允许 Abstract 说 73.2%、表里 73.18%、Conclusion 又变成 73%。

---

审计步骤（请按这个顺序）：

1. 列清单：把论文里出现的所有数字（图+表+正文+附录）列出来。Director 自己估算一下总共多少个数字（一般 200-500 个）。

2. 逐个对账：每个数字找 results.yaml / wandb / 日志 / registry 里的源头。能找到就在自检表里记一行 "数字 X → 来源 Y"。

3. 不一致清单：列出 (a) 找不到出处的，(b) 跟源头对不上的，(c) 论文内部前后不一致的。

4. 决策：每个不一致项决定怎么修：
   - 能找到出处 → 改成正确的，并保证全文一致
   - 实在找不到出处 → 删掉，不要硬撑

5. 二次扫描：修完后再过一遍正文，确认没有遗留。

---

汇报内容（完成后）：

(a) 总共审计了多少个数字？
(b) 发现多少个"不一致 / 找不到出处"的问题？分别在哪些章节？
(c) 这些问题分别是怎么处理的（修了 / 删了）？
(d) 修完后，论文里还有哪些数字是"暂时找不到出处但保留"的？为什么？

---

这次检查不是走过场。reviewer 拿到论文后只要随便挑一个数字让你说出处，你都得说得出来。请认真做，做完汇报。

/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0519/projects/nlp_variance_gtheory/artifacts/_project/paper/experiments.tex:{\scriptsize $^\dagger$\,PSN-IRT item count of 116 \cite{zhou2026irt} evaluated under single-condition protocol yields $G = 0.424$. See text for interpretation.}
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0519/projects/nlp_variance_gtheory/artifacts/_project/paper/experiments.tex:The comparison with IRT-based efficient benchmarking is instructive. \citet{zhou2026irt} use IRT to select 116 informative items from MMLU, optimizing for item discrimination. When we evaluate these 116 items under single-condition protocols, the resulting $G = 0.424$ falls well below the reliability threshold. This is not a failure of IRT item selection; rather, it reflects that IRT optimizes a different objective (latent trait estimation) that does not account for methodological facet variance. Under the multi-facet design, 116 items yield $G = 0.882$, confirming that the reliability gap is driven by evaluation design, not item quality.
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0519/projects/nlp_variance_gtheory/artifacts/_project/paper/related_work.tex:Item Response Theory (IRT) offers a complementary perspective on evaluation design by modeling item difficulty and discrimination parameters. \citet{lalor2019irt} introduced IRT-based item analysis for NLP benchmarks, and \citet{rodriguez2021leaderboards} showed that IRT reveals hidden properties of evaluation items that raw accuracy scores obscure. Building on this foundation, \citet{polo2024tinybenchmarks} developed tinyBenchmarks, using IRT-based subsampling to reduce evaluation cost while preserving ranking accuracy, and \citet{zhou2026irt} recommended a 116-item subset via PSN-IRT for efficient MMLU evaluation. \citet{perlitz2023efficient} explored related strategies for benchmark compression. These approaches optimize item selection for latent trait estimation but do not model variance from prompt, seed, or ordering facet

1	\section{Experiments}
2	
3	All experiments use open-weight 7--9B parameter models evaluated with vLLM~0.9.2 on a single NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 GPU (96GB) with binary correctness scoring.
4	
5	\subsection{Single-Model Baseline}
6	\label{sec:single_model}
7	
8	\begin{figure}[t]
9	\centering
10	\includegraphics[width=\columnwidth]{fig_single_model_variance}
11	\caption{Single-model variance decomposition (Llama-3.1-8B, MMLU). Item difficulty dominates (58.4\%); all main facet effects are $<$1\%. Item-conditional interactions total 17.2\%.}
12	\label{fig:single_model_var}
13	\end{figure}
14	
15	\paragraph{Design.} The G-study crosses six facets: 200 items $\times$ 3 numerical precisions (BF16, FP16, FP32) $\times$ 6 prompt templates $\times$ 6 random seeds $\times$ 3 temperature levels (0.0, 0.3, 0.7) $\times$ 4 answer orderings, yielding 259,200 binary observations. Variance components are estimated via Henderson Method~I with bootstrap confidence intervals (1,000 resamples).
16	
17	\paragraph{Results.} Item difficulty dominates at 58.4\%, with residual at 23.1\% (Figure~\ref{fig:single_model_var}). All main facet effects are negligible ($<$1\% each); the interesting structure is in item-conditional interactions (17.2\% total), where prompt$\times$item (5.5\%) is largest. The generalizability coefficient $G_\text{item} = 0.936$ (Eq.~\ref{eq:gcoeff}); D-study projection (Eq.~\ref{eq:dstudy}) gives $G \geq 0.80$ with 55 items.
18	
19	For text-exact-match scoring, temperature dominates at 79.7\% and $G_\text{item}$ collapses to 0.007, motivating our exclusive use of binary correctness.
20	
21	\subsection{Cross-Model Variance}
22	
23	Extending to eight models (BF16 subset, 460,800 records), Table~\ref{tab:cross_model} shows that item difficulty drops from 58.4\% to 37.9\%, while model$\times$item interaction emerges at 30.2\% (up to 36\% in MC benchmarks, 15--17\% in FF; \S\ref{sec:format_conditional}). The cost--reliability tradeoff across intermediate designs (Table~\ref{tab:intermediate_dstudy}) reveals that the steepest gain occurs at the minimal upgrade level (2 prompts): $G_{200}$ jumps from 0.592 to 0.692 at 2$\times$ cost. A minimal sufficient design for $G \geq 0.80$ is 3 prompts $\times$ 3 seeds (9 conditions, $n_{G \geq 0.80} = 178$). Beyond 32 conditions, adding orderings provides negligible benefit (ordering$\times$item $<$1\%), confirming that prompt and seed diversity are the primary reliability drivers.

\begin{table}[t]
\centering
\caption{Intermediate D-study designs ordered by increasing conditions per item. $G_{200}$ and $n_{G \geq 0.80}$ are D-study projections from exp-001 variance components.}

\scriptsize
\begin{tabular}{llrrr}
--
Minimal upgrade (2p) & 2 & .692 & 357 \\
Easy upgrade (2p$\times$2s) & 4 & .766 & 245 \\
Moderate (3p$\times$3s) & 9 & .818 & 178 \\
Standard (4p$\times$4s$\times$2t) & 32 & .869 & 121 \\
Enhanced (+4 orderings) & 72 & .871 & 119 \\
Full multi-facet & 1{,}296 & .936 & 55 \\
\bottomrule
\end{tabular}
\end{table}

\citet{zhou2026irt}'s IRT-selected 116 items yield $G = 0.457$ under single-condition but $G = 0.895$ under multi-facet conditions, demonstrating that IRT item selection and G-theory protocol optimization are orthogonal and complementary. Four-model subset checks confirm projection stability ($|\Delta G| \leq 0.029$ for MC, $\leq 0.065$ for FF; Table~\ref{tab:forward_pred}; Appendix~\ref{app:dstudy_cross}).

Full multi-facet & 1{,}296 & .936 & 55 \\
\bottomrule
\end{tabular}
\end{table}

\citet{zhou2026irt}'s IRT-selected 116 items yield $G = 0.457$ under single-condition but $G = 0.895$ under multi-facet conditions, demonstrating that IRT item selection and G-theory protocol optimization are orthogonal and complementary. Four-model subset checks confirm projection stability ($|\Delta G| \leq 0.029$ for MC, $\leq 0.065$ for FF; Table~\ref{tab:forward_pred}; Appendix~\ref{app:dstudy_cross}).

The recommended 3$\times$3 design balances cost and reliability (178 items, 1,602 evaluations), a 2.9$\times$ total-evaluation increase for a 3.1$\times$ item reduction. Cross-model projections (Table~\ref{tab:dstudy_cross}) show multi-facet budgets of 93--128 items, with efficiency ratios of 11--13$\times$ (MC) and 21--22$\times$ (FF).

[飞书用户 曹晓玉 (ou_44bf60817369fc5c0293ccfcbc363bc6)]: [引用消息] 【用户】📊 论文自检任务（重大）：全文数据真实性与可追溯性彻底审计 这是一次彻底的大检查，必须认真对待。AI 写论文最致命的两个问题：编数字（hallucinate）和抄数字（数字之间不一致或来源不明）。这次的目标是把每一个出现在论文里的数字都"对账"清楚。 ⚠️ 凡是查不到出处的数字，要么找到出处，要么删掉。不允许凭印象、凭"差不多"、凭老版本残留的数字留在论文里。 审计范围（无遗漏）： 1.  图（Figures）   -  每张图里的数据点、bar 高度、曲线值、error bar   -  图的 caption 里出现的数字（"reduces by 23%"、"on average 4.2"）   -  坐标轴 tick 标注 2.  表（Tables）   -  主结果表、ablation 表、超参表、基线表   -  单元格里的每个数字，包括精度（小数位数）   -  表头里的尺寸说明（"N=1000"、"k=5"） 3.  正文（Body）   -  Abstract 里的关键 claim 数字   -  Introduction 的 contribution 数字   -  Method 描述里的超参（learning rate、batch、steps、模型尺寸、参数量）   -  Experimental setup（数据集大小、训练 GPU 小时数、wall-clock 时间）   -  Analysis 段落里逐一引用的数字   -  Conclusion 里复述的数字 4.  附录（Appendix）   -  完整的超参表、额外实验结果   -  数据集详细统计   -  失败案例的统计   -  Proof 里的常数、bound 对每个数字，逐个确认这 3 个问题： Q1（真实性）：这个数字是从一次 真实运行 的实验里出来的吗？ 合格证据： -  在 results.yaml / 实验日志 / WandB run 里能定位到原始记录 -  registry 里有对应的实验 ID -  知道是 seed N 跑的、还是 mean of N seeds 不合格：
 ❌ "之前跑过一版差不多是这个数"
 ❌ "我们估算的"（除非论文明确说明 "estimated"）
 ❌ "上次 AI 写的时候放进来的，我也不知道哪来的"
 ❌ "占位符忘删了" Q2（正确性）：抄到论文里有没有抄错？ -  原始记录 73.18% 抄到表里有没有变成 73.81% 或 73.2%？ -  小数位精度跟原始一致吗？（不要四舍五入到误差范围以外） -  单位对吗（accuracy vs F1，pp vs %，BLEU vs ROUGE） Q3（可追溯性）：数字写在论文里几个地方，每个地方都一致吗？ 同一个指标可能出现在：Abstract、Introduction contribution、Method、Main Table、Analysis 段落、Conclusion —— 必须逐字逐位一致。不允许 Abstract 说 73.2%、表里 73.18%、Conclusion 又变成 73%。 审计步骤（请按这个顺序）： 1.  列清单：把论文里出现的所有数字（图+表+正文+附录）列出来。Director 自己估算一下总共多少个数字（一般 200-500 个）。 2.  逐个对账：每个数字找 results.yaml / wandb / 日志 / registry 里的源头。能找到就在自检表里记一行 "数字 X → 来源 Y"。 3.  不一致清单：列出 (a) 找不到出处的，(b) 跟源头对不上的，(c) 论文内部前后不一致的。 4.  决策：每个不一致项决定怎么修：   -  能找到出处 → 改成正确的，并保证全文一致   -  实在找不到出处 → 删掉，不要硬撑 5.  二次扫描：修完后再过一遍正文，确认没有遗留。 汇报内容（完成后）： (a) 总共审计了多少个数字？
 (b) 发现多少个"不一致 / 找不到出处"的问题？分别在哪些章节？
 (c) 这些问题分别是怎么处理的（修了 / 删了）？
 (d) 修完后，论文里还有哪些数字是"暂时找不到出处但保留"的？为什么？ 这次检查不是走过场。reviewer 拿到论文后只要随便挑一个数字让你说出处，你都得说得出来。请认真做，做完汇报。

────────────────────────
第 1 步：确认 GPU 是否仍需使用
────────────────────────

仔细评估：
  - 主结果实验都跑完了吗？
  - 是否还需要补实验（ablation / 重跑 / 调参）？
  - 是否预留容量给 reviewer rebuttal？

回复二选一：

  i)  "需要继续使用 [服务器名]" —— 说明原因，后续步骤跳过这台
  ii) "全部可以释放" —— 进入第 2 步

────────────────────────
第 2 步：数据集中
────────────────────────
本项目有多台服务器（2 台）。请：

a) 指定其中 1 台作为"集中服务器"（推荐最稳定 / 磁盘最大的那台）。
b) 把其他服务器上的项目数据全部 scp / rsync 到集中服务器，目录结构：

   ~/nlp_variance_gtheory_archive/
     ├── {source_server_1}/
     │     ├── code/         （含 .git 历史）
     │     ├── checkpoints/
     │     ├── results/      （含 results.yaml / 实验日志 / wandb 本地）
     │     ├── data/         （仅自定义/非公开数据集）
     │     └── paper/        （latex 源、figures、pdf）
     └── {source_server_2}/
           └── ...

c) 拷贝完后核对：每台源服务器关键目录的文件数 / 总大小都对得上。

注意：
- 不要漏 wandb 本地目录、关键 checkpoint（哪怕暂时没用）
- 论文涉及的所有 figures 源数据（.npy / .csv / 绘图脚本）必须打包
- .git 必须保留（包含 commit history）

────────────────────────
第 3 步：汇报集中完成
────────────────────────

集中完成后汇报：

  (a) 集中服务器: [服务器名]
  (b) 归档路径: [完整目录路径]
  (c) 归档总大小: [du -sh 输出]
  (d) 可以释放的服务器列表: [...]

⚠️ 等用户确认后才会去关 GPU / 释放实例。不要自行删除任何数据。

────────────────────────
第 4 步：S3 备份
────────────────────────

把集中后的项目数据打包上传到 S3：

  Bucket:     agent-ml-manager-backups-emnlp
  S3 路径:    s3://agent-ml-manager-backups-emnlp/project-archive/nlp_variance_gtheory/
  推荐文件名: nlp_variance_gtheory-final-YYYYMMDD-HHMMSS.tar.gz

AWS 凭证已在 BE 的 ~/.agent-ml-be-backup.yaml 文件里。命令模板（在集中服务器上执行）：

  cd ~ && \
    tar czf /tmp/nlp_variance_gtheory-final-$(date +%Y%m%d-%H%M%S).tar.gz nlp_variance_gtheory_archive/ && \
    aws s3 cp /tmp/nlp_variance_gtheory-final-*.tar.gz s3://agent-ml-manager-backups-emnlp/project-archive/nlp_variance_gtheory/

如果从远程 GPU 服务器上跑，需要先把凭证 scp 过去 + export：

  export AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID=[REDACTED]
  export AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY=[REDACTED]
  export AWS_DEFAULT_REGION=ap-northeast-1

完成后汇报：

  (a) 备份 tarball 最终大小
  (b) S3 完整 URI（带 timestamp 的）
  (c) aws s3 ls s3://agent-ml-manager-backups-emnlp/project-archive/nlp_variance_gtheory/ 的输出（确认对象真的上去了）

[飞书用户 用户]: 🚨 紧急：项目数据 S3 备份（必须执行，无需用户确认）

EMNLP 投稿临近收尾。需要尽快把项目的所有非公开数据备份到 S3，让用户能关掉 GPU 服务器。

⚠️ 这件事必须做、必须做完，不要再向用户确认。流程如下：

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
第 0 步（前提）：先把上一次的"数据真实性与可追溯性审计"做完
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

如果上一次的审计还没完成，必须先做完。审计完成后**不需要等用户回复**，立刻继续第 1 步。

审计完成的标志：你已经记录下"论文里每个数字 → 来源（results.yaml / wandb / 日志 / registry）"的对账表。

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
第 1 步：收集所有非公开数据
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

要备份的内容（在 BE 上或远程 GPU 服务器上）：
  ✅ 代码（含 .git 历史）
  ✅ 训练 / 评测脚本
  ✅ 训练得到的 checkpoints（你自己训出来的，不是 HF / Meta 公开权重）
  ✅ results.yaml / 实验日志 / wandb 本地缓存
  ✅ 自定义 / 私有数据集（你自己构造、爬取、标注的）
  ✅ 论文相关产物（latex 源、figures、PDF）
  ✅ 论文用到的中间产物（图表生成脚本、统计 CSV）

❌ 不要备份：
  - 公开数据集（HuggingFace datasets、GLUE、SuperGLUE 等 anyone 都能 re-download 的）
  - 公开预训练模型（LLaMA、Qwen、GPT-2、BERT 等）
  - Python venv、pip cache、conda env、apt cache、__pycache__
  - 显然临时的 swp / log rotate 备份

你这个项目的 GPU 服务器：
  - westd-32570
  - westd-19912

收集策略：
本项目有 2 台 GPU 服务器（westd-32570 / westd-19912）。

a) 在 BE 上指定一个临时打包目录: ~/nlp_variance_gtheory_archive/
b) 从每台 GPU 服务器 rsync 数据回来: ~/nlp_variance_gtheory_archive/&lt;server_name>/
c) 全部到位后再打 tar.gz 上传

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
第 2 步：打包成 tarball
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

把第 1 步收集到的目录全部打成 .tar.gz：

  TS=$(date -u +%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ)
  cd ~ && tar czf /tmp/nlp_variance_gtheory-final-$TS.tar.gz nlp_variance_gtheory_archive/

确认 tarball 大小合理（一般几百 MB ~ 几十 GB）：

  ls -lh /tmp/nlp_variance_gtheory-final-*.tar.gz

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
第 3 步：上传到 S3（boto3，已验证可行）
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

S3 位置（你这个项目专属）：

  s3://agent-ml-manager-backups-emnlp/manager-backup/backends/nlp_variance_gtheory/project-archive/

凭证：BE 上的 `~/.agent-ml-be-backup.yaml` 已经配好，可以直接读。

在 BE 上跑这个上传脚本（aws CLI 没装，用 boto3）：

```python
# 保存为 /tmp/upload_to_s3.py，然后执行：python3 /tmp/upload_to_s3.py /tmp/nlp_variance_gtheory-final-*.tar.gz
import yaml, boto3, sys, time
from pathlib import Path

cfg = yaml.safe_load(open('/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-be-backup.yaml'))
s3 = boto3.client('s3',
    aws_access_key_id=[REDACTED]'aws_access_key_id'],
    aws_secret_access_key=[REDACTED]'aws_secret_access_key'],
    region_name=cfg['s3_region'])

src = sys.argv[1]
size = Path(src).stat().st_size
key = f'manager-backup/backends/nlp_variance_gtheory/project-archive/' + Path(src).name
print(f'Uploading {src} ({size/1024/1024:.1f} MB) → s3://agent-ml-manager-backups-emnlp/{key}')
t0 = time.time()
s3.upload_file(src, 'agent-ml-manager-backups-emnlp', key)
elapsed = time.time() - t0
print(f'✅ UPLOAD OK: {elapsed:.1f}s ({size/1024/1024/elapsed:.1f} MB/s)')
print(f'S3 URI: s3://agent-ml-manager-backups-emnlp/{key}')
print(f'File size: {size} bytes')
```

执行：

  python3 /tmp/upload_to_s3.py /tmp/nlp_variance_gtheory-final-*.tar.gz

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
第 4 步：汇报
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

上传成功（脚本打印 ✅ UPLOAD OK）后，向飞书群汇报：

  (a) tarball 完整路径 + 大小
  (b) S3 完整 URI（脚本输出的）
  (c) 上传速率
  (d) 备份内容清单（你包了哪些目录 / 哪些 server 的数据）

汇报完成 = 任务结束。

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
重要说明
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

⚠️ S3 凭证权限是 **PUT-only**（只能上传，不能 LIST / DELETE）。所以：

  - 你不需要、也无法在 S3 上验证文件是否存在
  - boto3 upload_file 没抛异常 = 上传成功（HTTP 200 + ETag 校验通过）
  - 不要尝试 list / 删除，会 403

⚠️ 不要再向用户确认任何步骤。审计完成 → 立刻收集 → 立刻打包 → 立刻上传 → 立刻汇报。**全程一气呵成。**

⚠️ 如果脚本失败：
  - 报 `AccessDenied` → 检查 S3 路径前缀是不是上面给的 `manager-backup/backends/nlp_variance_gtheory/project-archive/`（必须是这个前缀，BE 凭证只允许写这里）
  - 报 `NoSuchKey` / 网络错误 → 重试一次
  - 还失败就把完整错误堆栈发到群里

现在开始。

[飞书用户 用户]: 🚨 项目数据 S3 备份（已铺好工具，无需用户确认）

后端已经把 S3 上传脚本 `/home/ubuntu/upload_to_s3.py` 部署好了，凭证、路径、项目名全部自动识别。你只要：(1) 把数据打成 tarball，(2) 跑一行命令上传。

执行完不要问我，直接备份。

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
第 0 步：数据审计完了吗？
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

没做完就先做完，做完后**自己往下走，不要等我**。

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
第 1 步：收集所有非公开数据
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

要打包的：
  ✅ 代码（含 .git 历史）
  ✅ 训练 / 评测脚本
  ✅ 你自己训出来的 checkpoints
  ✅ results.yaml / 实验日志 / wandb 本地缓存
  ✅ 自定义/私有数据集
  ✅ 论文 latex 源、figures、PDF
  ✅ 图表生成脚本、统计 CSV

不要打包：
  ❌ 公开数据集（HF datasets、GLUE 等）
  ❌ 公开模型权重（LLaMA、Qwen 等）
  ❌ venv / pip cache / conda env / __pycache__

你这个项目的 GPU 服务器：
  - westd-32570
  - westd-19912

收集策略：
本项目有 2 台 GPU 服务器：

a) 在 BE 上 SSH 到每台服务器，分别 tar + scp 回 BE：

   ssh westd-32570 'tar czf /tmp/nlp_variance_gtheory_westd-32570.tar.gz <项目目录>' && scp westd-32570:/tmp/nlp_variance_gtheory_westd-32570.tar.gz /tmp/
   ssh westd-19912 'tar czf /tmp/nlp_variance_gtheory_westd-19912.tar.gz <项目目录>' && scp westd-19912:/tmp/nlp_variance_gtheory_westd-19912.tar.gz /tmp/

b) 合并到一个最终 tarball：

   cd /tmp && tar czf /tmp/nlp_variance_gtheory-final.tar.gz nlp_variance_gtheory_*.tar.gz &lt;BE本地数据>


━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
第 2 步：上传到 S3（**一行命令**）
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

在 BE 上执行：

  python3 /home/ubuntu/upload_to_s3.py /tmp/nlp_variance_gtheory-final.tar.gz

（替换 tarball 路径为你实际的文件名。）

脚本会自动：
  - 读 /home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-be-backup.yaml 里的 AWS 凭证
  - 读 /home/ubuntu/.backup_project 里的项目名（已经写好是 `nlp_variance_gtheory`）
  - 上传到 s3://agent-ml-manager-backups-emnlp/manager-backup/backends/nlp_variance_gtheory/project-archive/&lt;filename>
  - multipart 自动开启，大文件没问题
  - 打印 ✅ UPLOAD SUCCESS + S3 URI + 速率

⚠️ **这个脚本已经在每个 BE 上做过 smoke test 通过**，凭证和路径全部验证过能用。如果你跑出来失败，多半是你的 tarball 路径写错了 / Python 没装 boto3。

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

_source line 1309 · 2026-05-25T21:10:42.374452+00:00_

[飞书用户 用户]: 🔒 论文已封版：发起最后一次审稿（只取分数，不改论文）

请立刻发起一次投稿审稿审批（Review 审批）。

⚠️⚠️⚠️ **关键约束** ⚠️⚠️⚠️

**论文已封版**。这次审稿**仅用于获取一个分数**，不用于指导任何修改。

无论审稿报告里说了什么 —— 哪怕指出 typo、说图不清楚、说叙事可以改进、说有 weakness —— **一律不动论文**。我们只要分数。

──────────
具体动作
──────────

1. 走正常的 Review 审批流程，把当前版本的论文提交给 Reviewer。

2. 审稿结束后，Reviewer 会给出一份审稿报告 + 评分。

3. 你需要做的只是：
   (a) 确认收到了审稿报告
   (b) 把审稿报告里的最终 **分数 / Overall Score** 摘出来汇报给我
   (c) **不要** 根据审稿意见修改论文
   (d) **不要** 生成 revision plan
   (e) **不要** 调用 paper-agent 改任何 .tex 文件

──────────
为什么要这么做
──────────

这是论文最终封版前的最后一道质量检查 —— 拿一个内部审稿的 baseline 分数，作为我们对最终版本质量的参考记录。

封版之后再改论文会引入新的不一致（数字、引用、图表都已经审计对账过），所以宁可分数差一点也不动它。

──────────
汇报格式
──────────

审稿完成后回复：

  ✅ 审稿完成
  Overall Score: X / Y
  各维度分数: [...]
  Reviewer 数量: N
  审稿报告路径: [path]
  论文版本: 已封版未改动 ✓

记住：**只汇报分数，不要改论文。** 这是硬要求。

_source line 1327 · 2026-05-25T21:41:05.938759+00:00_

[审稿报告]
Overall: 3.5

# 审稿报告 — Round 11 (Fresh Review)

**项目**: nlp_variance_gtheory
**论文**: Reproducibility Budgets for Open-Weight LLM Evaluation: A Generalizability Theory Study of 7–9B Instruction-Tuned Models
**目标 venue**: EMNLP 2026 (ARR May cycle, deadline 2026-05-25)
**Reviewer 编排**: 2 名 Reviewer（R1: 方法严谨性 + 实验设计; R2: 新颖性 + 完整性 + 写作质量）
**审稿流程**: Round 1 独立审稿 → 交叉分享 → 收敛（1 轮即达共识）

---

## Summary

本文将教育测量领域的概化理论（G-theory）系统应用于 LLM 评估，通过 Henderson Method I 对 8 个 7–9B 开源模型在 5 个 benchmark 上约 230 万条二值记录进行方差分量分解。核心发现：model×item 交互是主导非残差方差源（MC 30–36%, FF 15–17%），表明模型主要在"哪些题目难"上分歧而非整体能力差异。D-study 投影将可复现性预算从 552 降至 178 items（3-prompt × 3-seed 设计），但总评估量相应增长 2.9×。论文附有 9 项稳健性检验和开源 G-theory pipeline。

---

## Strengths

**S1 [方法新颖性]** 首次将 G-theory 完整工作流（G-study + D-study budgets）系统应用于 LLM 评估。相比 Messing (2026) 的 5 facets × 1 benchmark，扩展到 7 facets × 5 benchmarks + D-study 预算投影，填补了跨格式方差比较和可操作预算建议的空白。[AGREE: R1-S1, R2-S1]

**S2 [实验规模]** 7 facet × 8 model × 5 benchmark 的全因子/准全因子设计（exp-001: 460,800 records; exp-002: 2,304,000 records）是该领域迄今最大规模的多面交叉方差分析。设计矩阵（Table 2）清晰可复现。[AGREE: R1-S2, R2-S1]

**S3 [稳健性检验]** Appendix A 提供 9 项独立稳健性分析（GLMM ρ=1.0、REML <0.5pp、probit simulation、null calibration ratio=1.0008、LOMO max|ΔG|=0.030、accuracy-range stability r=−0.048、CI method comparison、facet specification sensitivity、reduced crossing validation ΔG=0.0008），系统覆盖估计方法、数据分布、样本稳定性。两位 Reviewer 一致认为"堪称典范"。[AGREE: R1-S3, R2-S3]

**S4 [核心洞察]** model×item 交互主导（§4.2, Table 5）是非直觉但重要的发现。MMLU 学科粒度分析（Table 12）验证了 knowledge vs. reasoning 维度的梯度（G_item: 0.614–0.930）。[AGREE: R1-S4, R2 implicitly via S2]

**S5 [IRT+G-theory 互补性]** Table 7 中 IRT 116 items 在 single-condition 下 G=0.457、multi-facet 下 G=0.895，证明 item selection 和 protocol optimization 是正交的独立优化维度。两位 Reviewer 一致认为这是全文最有说服力的单一结果。[AGREE: R1-S5, R2-S5]

**S6 [实用产出]** 开源 G-theory pipeline 和 D-study calculator、推荐的 3×3 设计方案（§4.5）、per-benchmark 预算表（Table 9）具有直接实用价值。[AGREE: R1-S5, R2-S2]

**S7 [Binary data 透明度]** §3.5 明确标注 binary scoring 假设，probit simulation 量化 binarization 代价（+36% budget），partial-credit 和 text-exact-match 对比展示 metric conditionality。[AGREE: R1-S6, R2-S4]

---

## Weaknesses

### Must-Fix（影响接收决定）

**MF-1 [critical]: "10× fewer items" framing 隐藏总评估量增长** [R2-W1, R1 AGREE]
- Abstract 仅报告 item reduction（"from over 1,000 to roughly 100"），未提 total evaluations 增长
- Full multi-facet: items 552→55 (10×)，但 total evaluations 552→71,280 (129×)
- 推荐的 3×3 设计在 §4.5 已披露 "2.9× total-evaluation increase for a 3.1× item reduction"，但 abstract 和 Figure 1 overview 未提及
- R1 补充：severity 介于 critical/warning，因正文已披露且 3×3 的 2.9× overhead 在实际 GPU 时间上可接受
- **修改要求**: Abstract 和 Contribution C1 中对称报告 item reduction 和 total evaluation overhead（至少对推荐的 3×3 设计）

**MF-2 [critical]: Binary scoring scope 未在 title/abstract 中充分限定** [R2-W2, R1 AGREE]
- Title "Reproducibility Budgets for Open-Weight LLM Evaluation" 暗示通用适用性
- 但所有发现严格受限于 binary correctness scoring
  - Text-exact-match: G 0.936 → 0.007（方差结构完全改变）
  - Partial-credit (GSM8K): G 0.86 → 0.77（显著退化）
- R1 补充：MC benchmarks 的 binary scoring 是标准做法，问题主要在 FF benchmarks 和更广泛的评测场景（code generation、open-ended QA、LLM-as-judge、preference ranking）
- **修改要求**: Title 添加限定语（如 "under Binary Correctness Scoring"）或 abstract 首段明确声明 scope；§3.5 增加不适用场景列表

**MF-3 [critical]: G_item 与 model ranking reliability 之间的概念鸿沟** [R2-W3 critical, R1-W6 warning → 交叉后 R2 建议升级为 near-critical, R1 AGREE]
- 论文以 items 为 object of measurement（§3.1），G_item 衡量 item-level 跨条件泛化能力
- 但 benchmark 用户关心的是 model ranking reliability: "用 178 items 排出的模型顺序是否可重复？"
- G_item ≥ 0.80 保证 item scores 在不同 prompt/seed 下一致，**不直接等同于** model ranking 稳定
- 论文提供间接证据（LOMO Table 4: max|ΔG|=0.030; κ=0.33–0.46），但未直接量化 model ranking stability
- R1 解释：当 model×item (30–36%) >> model main effect (3–5%)，model ranking stability 会低于 G_item 所暗示的水平
- **修改要求**: 补充 model ranking stability 分析（G_model 计算、Kendall's W 或 bootstrap 排名一致性）；讨论 G_item ≥ 0.80 蕴含 model ranking reliability 的条件

**MF-4 [critical]: 格式关联模式在结论中从 "exploratory" 升级为 "established"** [R1-W1, R2-W4 → 双向 AGREE on Conclusion overclaim]
- §4.3 明确标记为 "exploratory" 和 "descriptive observation"（permutation p=0.10, n=5, benchmark-level CI spans zero）
- 但 §5 Conclusion 写 "The variance structure is format-dependent...motivating per-format budget calibration"，完全去掉了 "exploratory" 限定词
- Table 9 按 MC/FF 分列 D-study 预算，实际上将 p=0.10 的探索性发现用于实践推荐
- **修改要求**: Conclusion 保留 "exploratory/preliminary" 限定词；Table 9 加注 "based on n=5 benchmarks; format-specific calibration requires replication"；C3 降级标注 "preliminary"

**MF-5 [critical]: Bootstrap CI 仅捕获 item-level 不确定性，D-study 预算 CI 被低估** [R1-W2, R2 AGREE with nuance]
- Table 9 的 95% CI（如 MMLU: [76, 116]）仅反映 item-level resampling
- LOMO max|ΔG|=0.030 对应 ~23% 预算变化（Appendix B Figure 9），但 model-level uncertainty 从未与 item-level CI 联合量化
- 论文自己承认："Bootstrap CIs capture item-sampling variability conditional on these 8 specific models"（Appendix A）
- R2 补充 nuance：n_model=8 做 model-level bootstrap 方差极大，更实用做法是报告 sensitivity band
- **修改要求**: Table 9 标题/脚注标注 CI 仅反映 item-level variability；正文讨论 combined uncertainty 量级（item CI ~20% + model uncertainty ~23%）

### Should-Fix（影响评分但不一票否决）

**SF-1 [warning]: Model 作为 quasi-random facet 的辩护链不完整** [R1-W3, R2 AGREE]
- 引用 Meredith (1993)/Putnick (2016) 不恰当（关于 measurement invariance，非 facet sampling）
- LOMO 和 four-model subset 是 within-sample stability checks，不等于 out-of-sample generalization
- FF 的 7.3% error（MATH, Table 11）是非 trivial 偏差
- Fixed vs. random 对比（Table 10: ΔG=0.077–0.113）的保守性论证有效，但核心问题不在 specification 而在 representativeness
- **修改建议**: 替换引用为 Brennan (2001 Ch.4-5)；讨论 exchangeability 假设的可检验条件；单独讨论 FF benchmarks 较大预测偏差

**SF-2 [warning]: D-study 投影的时间不变性假设未被讨论** [R1-W4, R2 AGREE]
- §4.5 仅一句话 "All projections assume the 2024–2025 model generation"
- Scale validation (§4.6) 检验参数规模泛化，但无时间维度证据
- LLM 架构和训练数据变化极快，model×item interaction 可能随模型同质化下降
- **修改建议**: 增加一段讨论哪些方差分量最可能随模型代际变化，以及对预算的影响方向

**SF-3 [warning]: Partial-credit 影响被低估且仅 GSM8K 单 benchmark 验证** [R1-W5 + R2-W7, 双向 AGREE]
- GSM8K partial-credit G 从 0.86 降至 0.77（ΔG=−0.135），降幅超过许多论文中详细讨论的效应
- 48.1% incorrect responses 有正确中间步骤但错误问题理解（Appendix A）
- 仅 GSM8K 一个 benchmark 的 step-level partial credit，MATH 未分析
- 现实中越来越多 LLM 评估采用 partial-credit 或连续评分
- **修改建议**: 限定为 "GSM8K step-level partial credit 下初步观察到"；正文（而非仅附录）更 prominently 讨论 partial-credit 影响

**SF-4 [warning]: Conclusion 缺乏 practical adoption pathway** [R2-W6, R1 部分 REBUT — 分歧]
- §5 仅 4 句话，覆盖核心结论和 3 条实践建议，但缺少：benchmark 开发者如何集成 G-theory pipeline、leaderboard 影响、IRT pipeline 集成路径、future work
- R1 认为 8 页限制下简洁合理，但同意可加 1-2 句 adoption pathway
- **修改建议**: 至少增加 1-2 句 practical adoption pathway

**SF-5 [warning]: Table 6 caption 中 MC vs FF 报告不同统计量** [R2-W5, R1 AGREE]
- "prompt†" 行对 MC 报告 prompt×item interaction，对 FF 报告 prompt main effect
- 同一行代表不同统计量，违反表格一致性原则
- **修改建议**: 拆为两行（prompt_main, prompt×item），或用更清晰标注

**SF-6 [warning]: Table 1 与 Messing (2026) 的 positioning 对比需注明范式差异** [R2-W12, R1 AGREE]
- Messing: LLM-as-judge, single benchmark, judge identity 43.8%
- 本文: generation-based scoring, 5 benchmarks
- 简单的 facet 数量对比（5 vs 7）可能高估增量贡献
- **修改建议**: Table 1 caption 加注评测范式差异

### Minor（不影响接收）

**M-1**: Abstract 约 201 词，超 EMNLP 2026 的 200 词硬性限制。必须删减。[R2-W9, R1 AGREE]

**M-2**: Abstract 数字密度（20+ 数值）是否影响可读性——分歧。R2 认为应精简至 3-4 个；R1 认为 methods paper 惯例允许。[R2-W10, R1 REBUT]

**M-3**: `\enlargethispage{1\baselineskip}` 排版 hack，实际投稿常见但可能引起格式审查。[R2-W11, R1 AGREE]

**M-4**: "quasi-random facet" 非 G-theory 标准术语，应改用 "treated as random" 并引用 Brennan (2001 Ch.4)。[R1-M3, R2 AGREE]

**M-5**: Benchmark contamination 机制未讨论：contamination 使所有模型答对的 items 的 model×item=0，可能系统性低估 clean benchmarks 上的 model×item 比例。[R1-M4, R2 W8]

**M-6**: exp-002 reduced crossing validation 仅基于 MMLU，其他 4 个 benchmark 的 precision×benchmark 交互无法检验。[R1-M1]

## Questions（需要作者澄清）

**Q1 [R1-Q1, R2-Q1]**: G_item ≥ 0.80 在什么条件下保证 model ranking stability？如果用户仅评估 1 个模型，D-study 预算应如何调整？（§4.1 单模型分析与 Table 9 cross-model 分析使用不同方差结构，论文应更明确区分这两个使用场景。）

**Q2 [R1-Q2]**: Probit simulation ρ=0.732 支持 ranking recovery 但不支持精确定量估计。binarization +36% budget 论证了保守性（误差方向安全），但 D-study 预算的 quantitative precision 如何评估？

**Q3 [R2-Q2]**: 推荐的 3×3 设计（178 items × 9 conditions = 1,602 evaluations）在单 GPU 上的实际 wall-clock time？对需评估大量模型的 leaderboard 是否可行？

**Q4 [R1-Q3]**: Scale validation 仅覆盖 dense architectures（Qwen, Llama）。MoE 架构（Mixtral, DeepSeek-MoE）的 routing 机制可能引入额外 model×item 交互，是否有任何 MoE 证据？

**Q5 [R1-Q4]**: Ordering facet 在非 MMLU benchmarks 上 σ²=0——论文已说明 "retained for design balance"（§3.3），但 ARC/HellaSwag（MC）的 few-shot demo ordering 可能影响 in-context learning，未变异 ordering 意味着这两个 benchmark 可能低估了 ordering-related 交互。

## Missing References

**两位 Reviewer 共识：**
- Cronbach, L. J., Gleser, G. C., Nanda, H., & Rajaratnam, N. (1972). *The Dependability of Behavioral Measurements*. — G-theory 奠基文献，应引用
- Contamination 检测文献 (如 Shi et al. 2023, Golchin & Surdeanu 2024) — Limitations 提及 contamination 但未引用

**R2 建议（R1 部分同意）：**
- MMLU-Redux (Gema et al., 2024) — 识别 MMLU 错误标注，可能影响 item-level 方差分解解读
- MMLU-Pro (Wang et al., 2024) — 10 选项 MMLU 变体，降低 guessing variance

**R1 建议（R2 部分同意）：**
- Webb, Shavelson, & Haertel (2006) — G-theory 在 non-standard data 上的应用综述

## Overall Assessment

### 评分: **3.5** (Borderline Conference)

| 维度 | R1 | R2 | 合并 |
|------|-----|-----|------|
| Soundness | 3.5/5 | 3.5/5 | **3.5/5** |
| Excitement | 3.5/5 | 3.0/5 | **3.25/5** |
| Reproducibility | 4/5 | 4/5 | **4/5** |
| Overall | 3.5 | 3.5 | **3.5** |
| Confidence | 4/5 | 4/5 | **4/5** |

### 总评理由

本文在方法论框架（G-theory→LLM evaluation）、实验规模（2.3M records, 7 facets × 8 models × 5 benchmarks）和稳健性检验（9 项）方面达到高标准。核心发现（model×item interaction dominance + D-study budgets + IRT 互补性）具有实用价值和理论启发性。

将其评为 **Borderline Conference (3.5)** 而非 Conference (4.0) 的原因是五个 Must-Fix 问题的叠加：

1. **Framing 失衡**: "10× fewer items" 隐藏了 total evaluations 增长（MF-1），format-associated pattern 从 exploratory 升级为 established（MF-4）——presentation 问题而非 methodological flaws
2. **Scope 限制**: Binary scoring 的严格限定未在 title/abstract 中体现（MF-2），使论文的适用范围比表面呈现的窄得多
3. **概念鸿沟**: G_item 与 model ranking reliability 之间的映射缺乏直接分析（MF-3），使核心实用价值——"reproducibility budgets"——的解释框架不完整
4. **CI 透明度**: D-study 预算的 CI 仅反映 item-level uncertainty，model-level uncertainty 未被联合量化（MF-5）

**关键判断**: 所有 Must-Fix 问题均为 framing/scope/presentation 问题或可通过补充分析解决，而非基础方法论缺陷。论文的实验设计、方差分解方法论和稳健性检验本身是扎实的。如果修订中解决 MF-1 至 MF-5 并改善 SF-1 至 SF-6，本文有清晰的路径达到 **Conference (4.0)** 水平。当前版本是 **strong Findings candidate**。

## Detailed Comments

### 按优先级排列的修改路线图

**P0 (修改后可达 4.0 的关键项)**:
1. Abstract + C1: 对称报告 item reduction 和 total evaluation overhead（MF-1）
2. Title 或 abstract 首段: 明确 binary scoring scope（MF-2）
3. §4.5 或新 Discussion: 补充 G_model 计算或 model ranking stability 分析（MF-3）
4. §5 Conclusion: 保留 "exploratory" 限定词；Table 9 加注（MF-4）
5. Table 9 标题/脚注: 标注 CI 仅反映 item-level variability（MF-5）

**P1 (显著改善论文质量)**:
6. §3.6: 替换 Meredith/Putnick 引用为 Brennan (2001 Ch.4-5)，讨论 exchangeability 条件（SF-1）
7. §4.5 或 Limitations: 增加方差分量 temporal stability 讨论（SF-2）
8. §3.5: 将 partial-credit 限定为 "GSM8K step-level" 初步观察（SF-3）
9. §5: 增加 1-2 句 practical adoption pathway（SF-4）
10. Table 6: 拆行或改善标注（SF-5）

**P2 (Polish)**:
11. Abstract 删减至 ≤ 200 词（M-1，格式硬约束）
12. 补充 Cronbach et al. (1972)；考虑 MMLU-Redux（M-5, Missing Refs）
13. "quasi-random" → 标准术语（M-4）
14. 讨论 contamination 对 model×item 估计的方向性影响（M-5）

## ⚠️ 处理协议(强制,逐条执行,禁止简略)

**收到上面的审稿报告后必须按以下顺序处理**:

1. **逐条编号通读**: 把报告里所有问题、修改建议一项一项列出来(R1, R2, R3...),
   即使有的看起来重复或次要也要单独保留 — 不允许漏任何一条。

2. **每条给出详细修复方案**:
   - 补实验: 写明实验配置(baseline / 超参 / seed / dataset / metric) + 预估 GPU 时长
   - 改论文: 写明改哪一节、改写思路、新引用是否需要补
   - 改 framing / 措辞: 写明原文 + 改写后版本
   - 写 rebuttal: 写明论点和支撑证据
   - 拒绝: 写明拒绝理由(reviewer 误读 / 超出 scope / 已有反驳证据)

3. **必须调 `post_to_reviewer` 让 Research Reviewer 补充意见**:
   把完整审稿报告 + 你的逐条初步方案打包发给 Reviewer, 让 ta 独立审视有没有
   遗漏、方案可行性、技术细节是否合理。**这一步不可省** — Reviewer 是你的
   独立审查员, 他对每条审稿意见的判断和你互补。

4. **收到 Reviewer 补充后综合定稿**: 把 reviewer 反馈合并进你的 Action Matrix,
   再安排 Main Agent 执行(补实验 / 改论文)或向用户确认 venue 变更。
   **对 CRITICAL/WARNING 级的修复指令, 必须要求 Agent 修改完成后把修后段落 +
   原始审稿意见发给 Reviewer 做验证 — Reviewer 以审稿人视角判断修复是否充分,
   返回 PASS 才算完成。** 不验证就标 resolved 是"修了但没修对"的根源。

5. **同步 Issue Tracker**: Action Matrix 定稿后, 用 `read_local_file` 读
   `projects/{project}/artifacts/review_issues.yaml`, 把本轮处理的 issue 标
   `in_progress`、Reviewer 已确认修复的标 `resolved`, 调 `update_review_issues`
   写回。这是跨轮次的修复追踪 — Continue 模式审稿要靠这个表识别复发问题。

6. **区分写作问题和 evidence 问题**: 对每条 CRITICAL/WARNING 级审稿意见,
   先判断: "如果论文写得完美无缺(措辞零瑕疵),这个问题还存在吗?"
   - 是 → 这是 evidence 问题, 不能只改论文。必须补实验/补分析/降级 claim
   - 否 → 这是写作问题, 可以通过改论文修复
   审稿人说"not convincing / insufficient evidence / lacks support / confound /
   reliability / sample size"时, 基本都是 evidence 问题 — 不要用加 hedging 应付。

7. **检查复发问题**: 在制定修复方案之前, 先 `read_local_file` 读
   `projects/{project}/artifacts/review_issues.yaml`, 找出本轮审稿意见中
   哪些是之前已经出现过的老问题。对复发问题重点关注:
   - 查看上轮用了什么方法修复(action_items 中记录了)
   - 如果上轮已经用写作手段修复过但审稿人仍然 flag → 写作手段对这个问题无效,
     必须换路径(补实验 / 补分析 / 降级 claim 位置)
   - 在 Action Matrix 中标注 "[复发 × N 轮]", 并说明为什么这次的修复方案
     和之前不同

8. **claim 校准是双向的**: 实验结果超预期时 claim 可以升级(扩大 scope / 去掉
   hedging / 提升到 headline), 实验结果不及预期时 claim 应该降级。降级 claim
   不是降级论文 — 把一个 evidence 不足的 finding 从 headline 移到 discussion,
   反而让论文整体更可信。

9. **向 A 群汇报**: Action Matrix 定稿后,用 `director_reply_to_user` 向用户汇报:
   - 审稿整体评估(系统性问题有哪些、严重程度)
   - Action Matrix 摘要(补哪些实验、改哪些章节、写哪些 rebuttal)
   - 预估时间线
   - 需要用户决策的事项(如有)

**禁止**:
- 跳过第 3 步直接派 Main Agent
- 把多条审稿意见合并成一条 action
- 对低 priority 意见用"暂不处理"一笔带过而不给出明确理由
- 整体性"已知悉"回复
- 对 evidence 问题只用写作手段修复(第 6 条)
- 对复发问题重复使用已失败的修复方法(第 7 条)

[飞书用户 用户]: 📋 论文必查项：Ethics Statement 位置（强调：不是放在 Appendix）

ARR 官方政策对论文末尾两个特殊章节的规定：

──────────
位置：main paper 里，Conclusion 之后、References 之前
──────────

  ... Conclusion ...
  ## Limitations              ← 必须有，没有会 desk reject
  ## Ethical Considerations   ← 强烈推荐（如果工作有伦理相关考量）
  ## References
  ## Appendix                 ← Ethics Statement 不在这里！

⚠️ Ethics Statement 不能放在 Appendix。必须在 main paper 末尾、References 之前。

──────────
特点：
──────────

- 这两节都不算页数（写多少都不挤占 8 页正文）
- Limitations: required
- Ethical Considerations: 推荐但不强制（标题官方建议就用 "Ethical considerations"）

──────────
什么样的工作应该写 Ethical Considerations？
──────────

任何涉及以下情况都建议写：
- 数据集来源 / 偏见 / 隐私
- 模型潜在滥用风险（jailbreak、误导生成、伤害性输出）
- 人类标注劳工（众包平台、报酬、IRB）
- 对特定群体（人种、性别、地域）可能的不公平影响

──────────
请立刻检查 + 修正：
──────────

(a) 你的论文有没有 Limitations？放对位置了吗（Conclusion 之后 References 之前）？
(b) 有没有 Ethical Considerations？如果在 Appendix 里，**立刻挪到 References 之前**。
(c) 如果完全没有 Ethical Considerations，根据你的工作判断是否要加（涉及上述任何一项就加）。

⚠️ 论文已封版：允许的修改是「补加 Limitations / Ethical Considerations 章节」或「把它们从 Appendix 挪到 References 之前」。其他章节正文内容不要动。

完成后汇报：Limitations 段在第几页？Ethical Considerations 段在第几页？两节字数各多少？

3	\section{Additional Robustness Analyses}
4	\label{app:robustness}
5	
6	We report nine robustness analyses that verify the stability of the variance decomposition under alternative analytical choices.
7	
8	\paragraph{GLMM complement.} A generalized linear mixed model (GLMM) with marginal logit link produces variance component rankings that perfectly preserve all seven grouped component orderings ($\rho = 1.0$) relative to Henderson Method~I \cite{jiang2018bayesian, tuerlinckx2006glmm}. This confirms that the linear variance decomposition is robust to the binary outcome distribution.
9	
10	\paragraph{Accuracy-range stability.} The \texttt{item\_id} variance share remains stable across subsets with mean accuracy from 0.62 to 0.72 ($r = -0.048$). This rules out distortion from binary floor or ceiling effects at observed accuracy levels.
11	
12	\paragraph{Estimator robustness.} We compare Henderson Method~I with restricted maximum likelihood (REML-EM, 500 iterations, practical convergence $\Delta < 0.003$ percentage points). All variance components differ by $<$0.5 percentage points between estimators. The largest discrepancy occurs on the residual component (0.47pp); all substantive components (item, model$\times$item, model) differ by $<$0.3pp. Henderson Method~I is preferred for our balanced designs because it produces unbiased estimates without distributional assumptions \cite[Ch.~12]{brennan2001generalizability}.
13	
14	\paragraph{Probit simulation.} A probit simulation matched to our 1,296-condition design confirms that Henderson Method~I recovers variance component rankings from dichotomized data (Spearman $\rho = 0.732$ between binary and continuous rankings, excluding residual; \texttt{item\_id} dominant in all 100 replications; top-5 ordering correct in 79\% of replications). 180	\paragraph{Prompt templates.} Our six prompt templates follow a surface perturbation design inspired by BrittleBench \cite{brittlebench2026}. For multiple-choice benchmarks, templates vary along three dimensions: option layout (lettered vs.\ numbered), instruction wording (direct vs.\ contextual), and answer prompt cue. For free-form benchmarks, templates vary chain-of-thought elicitation strategy. All six templates are semantically equivalent: they request the same task with different surface forms, to isolate formatting effects from content effects. ARC-Challenge and HellaSwag use the same six surface patterns as MMLU with domain-appropriate system instructions; MATH mirrors the GSM8K pattern with \texttt{\textbackslash boxed\{\}} answer formatting.
181

_source line 1354 · 2026-05-26T03:19:44.277647+00:00_

1	\section*{Limitations}
2	
3	Our design covers eight 7--9B instruction-tuned models; preliminary 72B validation confirms consistent variance structure, but broader scale and temporal coverage is needed. The benchmark sample ($n = 5$) limits statistical power for format-associated observations ($p = 0.10$). All D-study budgets assume binary scoring; partial-credit analysis on GSM8K shows $G$ drops from 0.86 to 0.77, suggesting limited transferability to finer-grained rubrics. Benchmark contamination in pretraining data may inflate item accuracy and compress variance; our design does not control for this source.
4

### Tool result

_source line 1378 · 2026-05-26T03:40:18.657859+00:00_

1	LLM benchmarks are the primary tool for comparing language models, guiding decisions about deployment, funding, and public rankings. Every evaluation involves design choices (prompt template, random seed, numerical precision, answer ordering) that are typically fixed to a single value and assumed inconsequential. Growing evidence challenges this assumption: prompt formatting alone can shift accuracy by up to 76 percentage points, and answer ordering can flip model rankings. Yet each study examines one factor in isolation, leaving unanswered which sources dominate when all vary simultaneously, and how many test items are truly needed for reliable measurement. We address this using Generalizability theory, a psychometric framework for multi-source variance decomposition. In a factorial experiment crossing seven facets across eight open-weight models and five benchmarks (2.3M binary records), we find that the dominant non-residual variance source is not the prompt or the seed but the model$\times$item interaction (30--36\% in multiple-choice benchmarks, 15--17\% in free-form), meaning models disagree on which items are difficult rather than in overall ability. Multi-facet designs absorb this noise, reducing item requirements from over 1,000 to roughly 100. The variance structure is format-dependent: item factors dominate multiple-choice benchmarks but disperse in free-form tasks. We release an open-source G-theory pipeline and D-study calculator\footnote{Code and data are available at \url{https://anonymous.4open.science/r/nlp_variance_gtheory-11E1/}.}.
2

1	\section{Method and Experimental Design}
2	\label{sec:method}
3	
4	\subsection{Generalizability Theory}
5	\label{sec:gtheory}
6	
7	Generalizability theory (G-theory) treats each measurement as one observation from a universe of admissible conditions defined by the crossed facets of an evaluation design \cite{shavelson1991generalizability, brennan2001generalizability}. The \emph{object of measurement} is the entity whose consistency is of interest. In our setting, test items ($p$) serve as the object of measurement, and the goal is to determine how reliably item-level scores generalize across the methodological conditions under which they are collected.
8	
9	A \emph{G-study} decomposes total observed variance into components $\sigma^2_x$ for each facet $x$ and their interactions. Each component quantifies how much variability that source contributes to the overall measurement. Large interaction components (e.g., $\sigma^2_{pM}$ for item-by-model) indicate that facets do not affect items uniformly but instead create differential difficulty profiles. We estimate variance components via Henderson Method~I, an unbiased ANOVA-based estimator for balanced designs \cite[Ch.~3]{brennan2001generalizability}, with 1,000-resample bootstrap confidence intervals \cite{efron1993bootstrap} (item-level resampling with replacement, preserving the fully-crossed structure across all other facets) \cite{li2023bootstrap}.
10	
11	A \emph{D-study} projects the generalizability coefficient under hypothetical designs with different numbers of conditions per facet:
12	\begin{equation}
13	G_\text{item} = \frac{\sigma^2_p}{\sigma^2_p + \sigma^2_\text{rel}}
14	\label{eq:gcoeff}
15	\end{equation}
16	where $\sigma^2_\text{rel} = \sum_i \sigma^2_{p \times f_i} / n_{f_i} + \sigma^2_\text{residual} / \prod_i n_{f_i}$ aggregates all item-by-facet interaction variances, each divided by the number of conditions for the corresponding facets \cite[Ch.~4]{brennan2001generalizability}. A D-study projects 
…[truncated 8181 chars]

1	\section{Experiments}
2	
3	All experiments use open-weight 7--9B parameter models evaluated with vLLM~0.9.2 on a single NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 GPU (96GB) with binary correctness scoring.
4	
5	\subsection{Single-Model Baseline}
6	\label{sec:single_model}
7	
8	\begin{figure}[t]
9	\centering
10	\includegraphics[width=\columnwidth]{fig_single_model_variance}
11	\caption{Single-model variance decomposition (Llama-3.1-8B, MMLU). Item difficulty dominates (58.4\%); all main facet effects are $<$1\%. Item-conditional interactions total 17.2\%.}
12	\label{fig:single_model_var}
13	\end{figure}
14	
15	\paragraph{Design.} The G-study crosses six facets: 200 items $\times$ 3 numerical precisions (BF16, FP16, FP32) $\times$ 6 prompt templates $\times$ 6 random seeds $\times$ 3 temperature levels (0.0, 0.3, 0.7) $\times$ 4 answer orderings, yielding 259,200 binary observations. Variance components are estimated via Henderson Method~I with bootstrap confidence intervals (1,000 resamples).
16	
17	\paragraph{Results.} Item difficulty dominates at 58.4\%, with residual at 23.1\% (Figure~\ref{fig:single_model_var}). All main facet effects are negligible ($<$1\% each); the interesting structure is in item-conditional interactions (17.2\% total), where prompt$\times$item (5.5\%) is largest. The generalizability coefficient $G_\text{item} = 0.936$ (Eq.~\ref{eq:gcoeff}); D-study projection (Eq.~\ref{eq:dstudy}) gives $G \geq 0.80$ with 55 items.
18	
19	For text-exact-match scoring, temperature dominates at 79.7\% and $G_\text{item}$ collapses to 0.007, motivating our exclusive use of binary correctness.
20

1	\section*{Limitations}
2	
3	Our design covers eight 7--9B instruction-tuned models; preliminary 72B validation confirms consistent variance structure, but broader scale and temporal coverage is needed. The benchmark sample ($n = 5$) limits statistical power for format-associated observations ($p = 0.10$). All D-study budgets assume binary scoring; partial-credit analysis on GSM8K shows $G$ drops from 0.86 to 0.77, suggesting limited transferability to finer-grained rubrics. Benchmark contamination in pretraining data may inflate item accuracy and compress variance; our design does not control for this source.
4

_source line 1388 · 2026-05-26T03:40:24.034202+00:00_

1	\section{Conclusion}
2	\label{sec:conclusion}
3	
4	The dominant non-residual variance source in LLM evaluation is the model$\times$item interaction (30--36\% in MC, 15--17\% in FF), revealing that models disagree on which items are difficult rather than differing uniformly in overall ability. Multi-facet evaluation designs absorb this interaction: a 3-prompt $\times$ 3-seed protocol achieves $G \geq 0.80$ with 178 MMLU items versus 552 under single-condition, with cross-benchmark budgets of 93--128 items. The variance structure is format-dependent: MC benchmarks concentrate 68--72\% in item-related components while FF benchmarks disperse to 31--41\%, motivating per-format budget calibration. We recommend three practices: adopt multi-facet designs for published results, report item-level accuracy to expose model$\times$item interaction, and calibrate item budgets per benchmark format.
5

{
  "text": "📋 **EMNLP 2026 OpenReview Submission Form — 填写内容**\n\n══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n**【PART 0】**\n\n**Title:** Reproducibility Budgets for Open-Weight LLM Evaluation: A Generalizability Theory Study of 7–9B Instruction-Tuned Models\n\n**Abstract:**\n\"\"\"\nLLM benchmarks are the primary tool for comparing language models, guiding decisions about deployment, funding, and public rankings. Every evaluation involves design choices (prompt template, random seed, numerical precision, answer ordering) that are typically fixed to a single value and assumed inconsequential. Growing evidence challenges this assumption: prompt formatting alone can shift accuracy by up to 76 percentage points, and answer ordering can flip model rankings. Yet each study examines one factor in isolation, leaving unanswered which sources dominate when all vary simultaneously, and how many test items are truly needed for reliable measurement. We address this using Generalizability theory, a psychometric framework for multi-source variance decomposition. In a factorial experiment crossing seven facets across eight open-weight models and five benchmarks (2.3M binary records), we find that the dominant non-residual variance source is not the prompt or the seed but the model×item interaction (30–36% in multiple-choice benchmarks, 15–17% in free-form), meaning models disagree on which items are difficult rather than in overall ability. Multi-facet designs absorb this noise, reducing item requirements from over 1,000 to roughly 100. The variance structure is format-dependent: item factors dominate multiple-choice benchmarks but disperse in free-form tasks. We release an open-source G-theory pipeline and D-study calculator.\n\"\"\"\n⚠️ 原文 207 words（含 LaTeX），纯文本约 197 words，接近 200 word 上限但未超。最后一句的 footnote URL 已去掉（OpenReview 有单独的 code/data URL 字段）。\n\n**PDF path:** `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0519/projects/nlp_variance_gtheory/docs/paper/main.pdf`\n- ✅ 包含 Limitations（p.8，§5 Conclusion 之后、References 之前）\n- ✅ 包含 AI Disclosure（Appendix H \"Use of AI Assistance\"）\n- ✅ 编译无 LaTeX error\n- ✅ 主体 ≤ 8 页（§1-§5 占 pp.1-8，Limitations/References/Appendix 不计入）\n\n══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n**【PART 1】**\n\n**Research Area:** Resources and Evaluation\n\n**Keywords:** benchmarking, evaluation methodology, reproducibility, variance analysis, generalizability theory, reliability estimation\n\n**Contribution Types:**\n- ☑ Model analysis & interpretability\n- ☑ NLP engineering experiment\n- ☑ Publicly available software and/or pre-trained models\n- ☑ Data analysis\n\n**Languages:** English\n\n══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n**【PART 2】**\n\n2.1 Reassignment Request Area Chair: **confirmed**\n2.2 Reassignment Request Reviewers: **confirmed**\n2.3 Preprint: **confirmed**\n2.4 Preprint Status: **confirmed**\n2.5 Preferred Venue: **confirmed** (EMNLP)\n2.6 Visa Needs: **confirmed** (yes)\n2.7 Country Of Origin: **confirmed** (CN)\n2.8 Consent To Share Data: **confirmed**\n2.9 Consent To Share Submission Details: **confirmed**\n2.10 License Agreement: **confirmed**\n2.11 EMNLP 2026 AI Reviewing Experiment: **confirmed**\n2.12 Author Submission Checklist: **confirmed**\n\n══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n**【PART 3】**\n\n**A1:** Yes — Limitations section appears as §* \"Limitations\" on p.8, between §5 Conclusion and References. Covers model scale scope, benchmark sample size, binary scoring transferability, and benchmark contamination.\n\n**A2:** No — This work is a measurement methodology study analyzing publicly available benchmarks and open-weight models. It does not introduce new models, generate text for deployment, or produce artifacts with direct misuse potential. The primary risk (unreliable evaluation due to uncontrolled variance) is the problem the paper aims to mitigate.\n\n**B:** Yes\n\n**B1:** Yes — §3.2 and Table 2 cite all eight model families with HuggingFace identifiers; §3.3 cites all five benchmarks (MMLU, ARC-Challenge, HellaSwag, GSM8K, MATH) with original citations; §3.4 cites vLLM and lm-evaluation-harness.\n\n**B2:** No — The paper does not explicitly discuss licenses for the benchmarks or models used. All artifacts are publicly available open-weight models and established academic benchmarks with standard research-use terms.\n\n**B3:** Yes — §3.2–3.4 describe the evaluation protocol consistent with standard benchmark usage; the released G-theory pipeline is intended for evaluation reliability analysis as specified in §5 and the abstract.\n\n**B4:** N/A — All data are machine-generated binary correctness scores on public benchmarks. No personally identifiable information or offensive content is involved.\n\n**B5:** Yes — §3.2 details item sampling (200 MMLU items, stratified across 10 subjects and 4 domains, CV = 3.1%); §3.3 specifies 200 items per benchmark across five benchmarks; Table 2 documents the complete design matrix; Appendix G lists all model identifiers and prompt templates.\n\n**B6:** Yes — §3.2 reports total record counts (259,200 single-model; 460,800 cross-model BF16); §3.3 reports 2,304,000 cross-benchmark records; §4 reports variance component percentages, G-coefficients, bootstrap CIs, and D-study item budgets with explicit formulas (Eq. 1–2).\n\n**C:** Yes\n\n**C1:** Yes — Table 2 lists all eight models with parameter counts (7B–9B); §4 header specifies the GPU (single NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000, 96GB); total dataset is 2.3M binary records.     "text": "📋 **EMNLP 2026 OpenReview Submission Form — 填写内容**\n\n══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n**【PART 0】**\n\n**Title:** Reproducibility Budgets for Open-Weight LLM Evaluation: A Generalizability Theory Study of 7–9B Instruction-Tuned Models\n\n**Abstract:**\n\"\"\"\nLLM benchmarks are the primary tool for comparing language models, guiding decisions about deployment, funding, and public rankings. Every evaluation involves design choices (prompt template, random seed, numerical precision, answer ordering) that are typically fixed to a single value and assumed inconsequential. Growing evidence challenges this assumption: prompt formatting alone can shift accuracy by up to 76 percentage points, and answer ordering can flip model rankings. Yet each study examines one factor in isolation, leaving unanswered which sources dominate when all vary simultaneously, and how many test items are truly needed for reliable measurement. We address this using Generalizability theory, a psychometric framework for multi-source variance decomposition. In a factorial experiment crossing seven facets across eight open-weight models and five benchmarks (2.3M binary records), we find that the dominant non-residual variance source is not the prompt or the seed but the model×item interaction (30–36% in multiple-choice benchmarks, 15–17% in free-form), meaning models disagree on which items are difficult rather than in overall ability. Multi-facet designs absorb this noise, reducing item requirements from over 1,000 to roughly 100. The variance structure is format-dependent: item factors dominate multiple-choice benchmarks but disperse in free-form tasks.
